### 2025-04-21 17:09 — Understanding Hinduism and Tamil Philosophy
- From: vishnu88varthan @gmail.com · To: 
-  Understanding Hinduism and Tamil Philosophy Part 1: Who Are Hindus? Hinduism, also called Sanatana Dharma ( சனாதன தர்மம் ), means "Eternal Way." It is one of the world’s oldest and most diverse spiritual paths. It’s not just one fixed religion but includes many beliefs, rituals, and ways of thinking. Who is a Hindu? A Hindu is someone who follows the principles of Sanatana Dharma, such as: Dharma ( தர்மம் ) – Living a righteous and moral life Karma ( கர்மா ) – The idea that actions have consequences Moksha ( மோட்சம் ) – Freedom from the cycle of birth and death Reincarnation ( பிறவி சுழற்சி )
